# Completed work, moved out of the roadmap and idea notebook (2026-10-09)

### Pet Says dance variety and scenes (2026-11-13)

[PR #1628](https://github.com/rjdunlap/neo/pull/1628), `claude/pet-says-dance-scenes`, slice 4 of the [dive](../deep-dives/pet-says.md#build-slices-in-order). Level 5 turns carry `dance: { tune, style, statue }` (`makeDances`, `logic.ts`): four tunes (bounce, sway, shimmy, march), each with its own beat (0.22 to 0.46 s) and pentatonic steps; five styles (bounce, sway, twirl, shimmy, robot), two suited to each tune and never the same twice running; five named statues (tree, ice cream, flamingo, star, teapot), four different per round, with mitten places on every friend (`statuePose`). Every tune is used once per round, so two dances never share one. The pet says the style at the start ("Bounce to the music!") and the statue at the freeze ("Freeze like a tree!"), and the statue is now held until the grown-up's arrow (it released after 0.6 s before), which resolves known gap 6. Every level's round plays in one of five scenes (meadow, beach, living room, snowy hill, evening; `SCENES` and `SCENE_EXTRAS`), picked by seed after the turns so a seed still deals the same moves. Ten new `says.style.*` and `says.statue.*` lines use device speech (no rendered clips); the old `says.dance` and `says.freeze-now` lines were removed, and their two clips are orphans the next `scripts/voice-clips/render.mjs` run deletes. [Game notes](../games/pet-says.md#design-notes).

### Pet Says more moves, tiers and counts (2026-11-13)

[PR #1625](https://github.com/rjdunlap/neo/pull/1625), `claude/pet-says-move-tiers`, slice 3 of the [dive](../deep-dives/pet-says.md#build-slices-in-order). Ten new moves in `TIERS` (`logic.ts`): social (peekaboo, pat-a-cake), walking (tiptoe, march), animal (hop like a frog, stretch like a cat, waddle like a duck, flap like a bird) and calm (sway like a tree, shake it out; the dive left these two in no tier, so a fifth tier keeps the tiers a partition of the 18 moves). Level 1 draws from base plus social only; levels 3 and 4 from all 18; level 13 gains peekaboo and pat-a-cake. At level 3, about 60% of pair turns ask for two or three beats of a move made of beats (clap, stomp, jump, march), said with the shared `count` line, never above five. "Manner" (a big jump, a little jump) is left for later. A new `eyes` pair serves peekaboo; ten new `says.move.*` lines use device speech. [Game notes](../games/pet-says.md#design-notes).

### Splish Splash slice 2, hint accounting (2026-11-13)

[PR #1624](https://github.com/rjdunlap/neo/pull/1624), `claude/splish-splash-hint-rule`, slice 2 of the [dive](../deep-dives/splish-splash.md#build-slices-in-order) (decision 5). On parts levels 3–8 the seven-second glow is free; the second counted miss on one question lights it and counts that question's one hint (`QuestionHelp`, `GLOW_AFTER = 7`, `MISSES_FOR_HINT = 2` in `logic.ts`; two new rule tests). A pausing toddler no longer steps down; `early` bath checks both at levels 3 and 4.

### Duck Pond level 13, One big duck, first at lap (2026-11-13)

[PR #1596](https://github.com/rjdunlap/neo/pull/1596) adds level 13 (`friends`) as lap's first level (order `[13, 12, 1, 2]`; `entryLevels` `[12, 13]`). One big duck (2.4 times the size) is on the pond from the start; a touch anywhere has it quack and cheer and brings a friend swimming in from alternating sides, with the count spoken and on the sign with dots, up to five. Nothing holds the first touch, a touch during a swim-in brings the next, a touch after the fifth quacks with a splash, and after four quiet seconds the big duck hops, counting nothing. The round ends with a quack chorus, "Five friends…" and confetti; no miss or hint can be reached. [Game notes](../games/duck-pond.md#design-notes).

### Roundup level 1 at lap: one animal at a time (2026-11-12)

[PR #1594](https://github.com/rjdunlap/neo/pull/1594), `claude/roundup-lap-one-at-a-time`: at lap, level 1 (tap mode) brings one animal out at a time (the `sequential` rule level 10 has); the next hops out with a sparkle 0.8 s after each goes home. The fifteen-second waiting glow and the thirty-second trot home still happen but count no hint at lap (`lapTap(plan, band)` in `logic.ts`). Toddler's level 1 is unchanged. The level 1 how-to step adds that at lap age the animals come out one at a time. [Game notes](../games/roundup.md#design-notes) and [verification](../verification/2026-11-12-roundup-level-1-at-lap-one-animal-at-a-time.md).

### Duckling Parade level 1 at lap: a touch quacks, ducklings come out one at a time (2026-11-12)

[PR #1593](https://github.com/rjdunlap/neo/pull/1593), `claude/duckling-parade-lap-quack`: at lap only (level 1 is in lap's window alone, so it changed in place for that band), Mama quacks and hops at every touch-down anywhere, sky included (throttled 0.25 s, also while the ducklings hop into the pond before the finale). One duckling waits on the meadow at the start; each next one pops out (hop, pop, puff of stars) 0.6 s after the one before joins, at the first waiting spot more than 180 units from Mama (`nextToShow` in `logic.ts`, with rule tests). The fifteen-second glow still shows but at lap counts no hint. Toddler's level 1 is unchanged (all three out from the start, waiting counts a hint). The `arcade` suite's parade loop takes `PARADE_BAND` and accepts `ARCADE_ONLY=parade`. [Game notes](../games/duckling-parade.md#design-notes) and [verification](../verification/2026-11-12-duckling-parade-level-1-at-lap-a-touch-quacks.md).

### Animal Snack level 1 opens with one big animal (2026-11-12)

[PR #1592](https://github.com/rjdunlap/neo/pull/1592), `claude/animal-snack-lap-big-animal`: level 1 (lap only, so changed in place; the `oneBig` plan, munch mode) starts with one big animal (scale 1.1 against 0.6). A layer over the whole meadow has a touch anywhere feed the nearest animal at any distance; a touch while one eats or a friend walks in squishes with a tick and counts nothing. The second and third friends walk in from the right after the second and fourth snacks, each with its call, and six snacks finish the round. New line `snack.big` (device speech, no rendered clip). [Game notes](../games/animal-snack.md#design-notes) and [verification](../verification/2026-11-12-animal-snack-level-1-one-big-animal-at-lap.md).

### Egg Catch level 12, One big hen, first at lap (2026-11-12)

[PR #1591](https://github.com/rjdunlap/neo/pull/1591) adds level 12 as lap's first level (lap order `[12, 1]`; `entryLevels` `[8, 9, 12]`, so a lap save at 12 starts toddler at its easiest level). One big hen stands on a straw nest; a touch anywhere has her lay at once with her note, and a touch while her egg falls gets a cluck, a squash and a puff of stars, counting nothing. The basket waits under her, so no miss or hint can be reached, and she breathes while ready. The other three hens flutter onto the shelf one per egg caught. [Game notes](../games/egg-catch.md#design-notes).

[Game notes](../games/melt-freeze.md) and [verification](../verification/2026-11-12-melt-freeze-level-5-one-big-ice-cube.md).

### Lap and toddler upright games, batch 5 (chunk 31, 2026-11-09)

Branch `claude/upright-lap-toddler-batch-5` sets `upright: true` on Bubble Pop, Sing-Along Barn, Animal Snack, Size Parade, Far Lights and Stamp Studio; the upright-ready list is 25 games. Bubble Pop (start box named `spawnBox`) and Size Parade fit as is. Animal Snack's `spotEdges`/`snackXs` run the feeding spot 160..w-20 on the narrow upright area so five 110-wide snacks stay apart. Sing-Along Barn's `rowXs` (row starts at 170 upright, clear of the pet) and `cardSpots` (up to ten song cards in two rows) moved to `logic.ts`. Far Lights had returned null (a dark screen) at every level upright; the strip and window now run full width below Home and "?", the window capped at 820 tall, the curtain tassel just below the strip's right end, and level 8's pair moves right; sideways is unchanged. Stamp Studio's `stampLayout` puts the paper at the left edge below Home (578 wide), Play and Undo in the right column, stamps in the bottom row and colours from x 175. The shell's `GameScene.placeTip` moves the lap and toddler grown-up tip below Home and "?" (y 220) upright, because the tip was wider than the 768 area and covered Home; sideways and iPad are unchanged.

The [verification entry](VERIFICATION-2026-11-09.md#2026-11-09--lap-and-toddler-upright-games-batch-5-chunk-31) records the checks: the new `upright-batch-5.test.ts` holds every game to the 100-unit minimum and at least 50 px for the smallest target at 390 × 844.

### Grown-up upright games batch 4 (chunk 31, 2026-11-09)

PR #1457 (`claude/upright-grownup-batch-4`) adds upright phone play for Log Roll, Marble Jump and Snowball Merge; the grown-up upright pool is now 19 games, every game that lists the grown-up band. Log Roll's layout moved into the exported `logRollLayout(v, floor)`: upright, the floor starts at y 230 (below Home and "?") and centres in the tall view, with stones up to 112 units (smallest 88 units on the 8-wide level 4 floor on a short view, 76 sideways). Snowball Merge's `snowballLayout(v)` grows upright cells to 160 (128 sideways). Marble Jump needed no layout change: `boardLayout` already fits. Sideways layouts are unchanged.

The [verification entry](VERIFICATION-2026-11-09.md#2026-11-09--grown-up-upright-games-batch-4-chunk-31-pr-1457) records the target sizes (smallest 51 px for Log Roll and Snowball Merge, 39.6 px for Marble Jump's 78-unit holes and buttons) and touch play.

### Grown-up upright games batch 3 (chunk 31, 2026-11-09)

[PR #1454](https://github.com/rjdunlap/neo/pull/1454) adds upright phone play for Block Garden, Logic Grid, Species Comeback and Pyramid Solitaire; the grown-up upright pool is now 16 games across seven kinds. Pyramid Solitaire (`pyramidLayout`) stacks the pyramid, status line and Undo/Hint/Start over in the middle of the tall view, buttons clear of the guide. Species Comeback (`comebackLayout`) starts the tree and the release-site phase below Home and "?", with the nests in a centered row and Spring under them. Logic Grid extracts `caseLayout` (level 7) and `slidingLayout` (level 8) and starts boards at y 214 below Home and "?" (level 5's two-block board too). Block Garden's `gardenLayout` keeps a bed that would reach under Home below it on a short phone. `upright: true` applies to every band, so the child levels of Logic Grid and Block Garden were screenshotted upright too.

The [verification entry](VERIFICATION-2026-11-09.md#2026-11-09--grown-up-upright-games-batch-3-chunk-31-pr-1454) records the target sizes (smallest 51 px for Pyramid Solitaire, 36.6 px for Block Garden's 72-unit beds, 32.5 px for the 64-unit squares and cards of Logic Grid and Species Comeback, as held sideways) and touch play.

### New-game scaffold (codebase item 11, 2026-11-07)

[PR #1399](https://github.com/rjdunlap/neo/pull/1399) adds `npm run new-game`, with templates for the typed module/lifecycle, Pixi-free ladder, pending rule tests, voice/how-to, browser test stub and game page. It validates source-defined metadata, appends one land entry and refreshes the content barrels; the registry discovers the folder. Dry-run writes nothing, existing files and symlinks are refused, voice alias collisions are checked, and failed writes roll back. The starter explicitly throws until implemented; it adds no production game. [Contributor guide](../NEW-GAME.md).

Validation: 20 focused generator tests; a temporary generated game passed typecheck, docs generation and six barrel/doc checks, then was removed. Full units (328 files, 6,187 passed / 5 skipped, before two added CLI/formatting cases) and build passed; the final focused tests cover those added cases and rollback changes. No browser/offline run: app behavior, assets and navigation are unchanged. [Verification](VERIFICATION-2026-11-07.md#2026-11-07--new-game-scaffold-codebase-item-11-pr-1399).

### Gadget Bench levels 5–6 Little Gate and New Box (inventions slice 4, 2026-11-06)

[PR #1336](https://github.com/rjdunlap/neo/pull/1336), [definition](../definitions/gadget-bench.md#levels-56-little-gate-and-new-box-definition). Level 5: a low garden arch ends the path; the familiar cart crosses the conditions, then any tall part (big wheels, umbrella, even the flag) stops at it, and a low part does the same job; each round holds four tray parts. Level 6: the pet's cart works for one neighbor and its parts ride home, then a new box (glow stones, a roller belt) meets the same path; glow stones at the front fail the Give, on top they work. School range 2–6. Gaps: the arch stop reuses the existing stop animation without the arch-top tap, part glow or roll-back; the glow for a tall part doing nothing speaks `bench.fail.gate`.

Checks are in [VERIFICATION](VERIFICATION-2026-11-06.md#2026-11-06--gadget-bench-levels-56-little-gate-and-new-box-pr-1336).

### Jelly Drums slice 2, names, twists and co-play (2026-11-03)

[PR #1155](https://github.com/rjdunlap/neo/pull/1155), [dive slice 2](../deep-dives/jelly-drums.md#build-slices-in-order) (fresh 4, 5, 9). Free play (levels 1-3) names a jelly now and then (`jelly.name.<color>`, `JellyNamer`: at most one in eight taps, never the same jelly twice in a row, never over a fan's answer or the finale) and says `jelly.lowhigh` once a round after she has played both the biggest and the littlest. Level 2: every fourth note a front-row fan (duck, bunny, cat, pig in turn) hops and answers in its own `sfx.animal` voice with a note in the jelly's color (`fanAnswering`). Level 3: three neighbouring jellies in order, up or down, send a rainbow star burst across the jellies and twinkle the garland (`isScale`, `ScaleWatch`: a run is used once). `twist` is a field on `PLANS`; nothing counts as a miss. How-to steps for levels 2 and 3 updated. Deviations: the sparkle only twinkles the garland (progress still counts notes, no bonus bulb, since nothing is a goal beyond the note count); the co-play line is one added sentence on the single lap/toddler `coplayHint` ("Hum the low one, then the high one, and see if {name} plays it."), because per-band co-play needs a shell type change (open).

Checks are in [VERIFICATION](VERIFICATION-2026-11-03.md#2026-11-03--jelly-drums-names-twists-and-co-play-dive-slice-2).

### Rule Machine levels 3-4, algebra-balance AB1 (2026-11-02)

[PR #1120](https://github.com/rjdunlap/neo/pull/1120), branch `claude/rule-machine-3-4`, batch rule-machine 2, [definition](../definitions/rule-machine.md): `rule-machine`'s school range is now 2-4. Level 3 "What does it do?" (rules +2, +3, -1): three cars go through with pads, a see-through window shows the counters change, then a shelf of three cards; a wrong card is one miss with the newest row ringed and read out, a second glows the right card. Level 4 "Covered machine" (x2, x3, +2, +4): the window is covered and the shelf is out from the start; feeding is never a miss, a card tap while more than one card fits is not judged, a card some row breaks is a miss, the last open card hangs, then one unfed car is sent and its prediction counts; Peek lifts the cover for one run and is the machine's one hint. Decisions: shelf cards are 120x120 (the definition said 140x120) so landscape fits the table and shelf beside the help button; `game-reviewer` fixes: the cover returns when the card hangs after an unused Peek, and the pet repeats level 2's ask after the card hangs. The browser suite `rulemachine` takes `RM_LEVELS`.

Checks are in [VERIFICATION](VERIFICATION-2026-11-02.md#2026-11-02--rule-machine-levels-34-algebra-balance-ab1-batch-rule-machine-2).

### Rule Machine levels 1-2, algebra-balance AB1 (2026-11-01)

[PR #1091](https://github.com/rjdunlap/neo/pull/1091), branch `claude/rule-machine-1-2`, batch rule-machine 1, [definition](../definitions/rule-machine.md): a new game, `rule-machine`, Puzzle Peaks' 14th (so the land now pages), pre-K level 1 "Hat machine" and school level 2 "One more goes in" (LEVELS prek 1-1, school 2-2; the school ceiling rises with batch rule-machine 2). Level 1 predicts the look of the next creature from three pictures, level 2 the count on three pads once two rows show; the first prediction on each machine is free, a counted wrong gives a spoken reason, a second glows, and the right answer rings green beside her pick. Level 9 *Whistle when it's full*: plans keyed by `level` (`planFor` still falls back for unknown IDs), mode `whistle` with 4 things needing 1–3; a full set wiggles the whistle, an early whistle strains and blinks the empty rings (`helpers.whistle.notfull`), a helper sent past full bounces off and walks back (`helpers.full`); no miss or hint (decision 2's default). Only the number pads miss; the round answer writes its number on the round box only. Seven new voice lines; the how-to level 10 line is bounded and a level 11 line added. ### Sing-Along Barn first slice (2026-10-12)

Built `sing-along-barn` ([PR #219](https://github.com/rjdunlap/neo/pull/219)) for lap, toddler, preschool and pre-K, in Music Mountain: **Old MacDonald only**. The melody is written by scale degree in C (C D E G A, with the pickup on the G below), so every note is already in the island's pentatonic set and the pentatonic-exception decision was not needed. A test asserts one note per sung syllable, so her taps line up with a grown-up singing. Tap anywhere and the tune moves one note on (a row of beads shows the line; the next bead breathes); the song is a short form (the full verse's moo-moo lines and repeated first line are left out; the how-to card and co-play tip say so): the opening is "Old MacDonald had a farm, E-I-E-I-O", then each verse brings a friend out of the barn to sing "And on that farm he had a …" and answer in its own voice, before it settles on the bottom row. Levels: 1 one friend (25 taps), 2 two friends, 3 she asks two of five friends in (barn windows light; the others leave) and they sing in the order she chose. A seeded draw picks the friends at levels 1–2. Nothing can be wrong: `misses` and `hints` are always 0 and nothing is offered to the tune plaque (a song copied along is not hers). Rules in `logic.ts` (`makeSong`, `PLANS`, `farmAnimals`, `nextInvite`); the ghost finger taps for each note and invites friends in turn.

Not built, still proposals: more tunes (the pentatonic-fit ones first: Hot Cross Buns, Mary Had a Little Lamb, Au Clair de la Lune; those needing F wait on the exception decision), picking who comes next each verse, repeating a phrase, a "which animal says moo?" mode that has real wrong answers, and a controller or couch form.

### Sing-Along Barn level 4 (2026-10-13)

Built level 4 of `sing-along-barn` ([PR #278](https://github.com/rjdunlap/neo/pull/278)); pre-K now plays levels 3–4, and lap, toddler and preschool are unchanged. Plan `{ verses: 3, invite: true, each: true }`: she taps anywhere to sing the opening, and at the end of the opening and of each verse the five friends come out and she picks who sings next (a free choice, never a miss; a stray tap while choosing does nothing). The song grows by the chosen friend's verse. Friends who have sung wait in their own slot of the row and only say hello when tapped. The round ends after the third verse with one sticker, misses 0 and hints 0. New voice line `barn.next`. Three Old MacDonald verses are 51 notes (the note-cap test is 51 for the longest song, still 40 for two verses), and a test asserts, for each tune, that the song with fewer verses is a prefix of the song with more. The how-to demonstration sings only two verses, since three took 72.3 s of the 75 s limit (two take 53.2 s). Still proposals: a free pick of the tune, the full verse with its moo-moo lines.

### Sing-Along Barn level 5 (2026-10-13)

Built level 5 of `sing-along-barn`; pre-K now plays levels 3–5, and lap, toddler and preschool are unchanged. Plan `{ verses: 3, invite: true, each: true, chooseTune: true }` (`SingPlan.chooseTune`, the fifth plan): she first chooses the song herself from two big cards, a cow for Old MacDonald and a hot cross bun for Hot Cross Buns (any pick is right, never a miss; the first notes sound when she picks, voice line `barn.tune`), and then the level-4 flow runs unchanged (she sings the opening, then chooses who sings next after each of three verses). `misses` and `hints` are 0. The how-to demonstration sings two verses (ghost-finger demo 56.3 s, inside the 75 s limit). Still open: the full Old MacDonald verse with its moo-moo lines, which needs the demonstration limit to allow it.

### Sing-Along Barn level 6 (2026-10-13)

Built level 6 of `sing-along-barn`; pre-K now plays levels 3–6, and lap, toddler and preschool are unchanged. Like level 5 (she chooses the song, then who sings after each verse) but with two friends, and Old MacDonald is the whole verse: after "E-I-E-I-O" comes "With a moo-moo here and a moo-moo there, here a moo, there a moo, everywhere a moo-moo", then "Old MacDonald had a farm, E-I-E-I-O" again, and the friend sounds on every moo (the syllable `~` is its own sound word). Code in `logic.ts` (`WITH_A`, `AND_A`, `HERE_A`, `THERE_A`, `EVERYWHERE`, `OLD_MACDONALD_FULL`, `SingPlan.full`, `tunesFor`), `index.ts` and the how-to step from level 6; 47 notes per friend, 59 for one, 106 for two; Hot Cross Buns is unchanged. The how-to demonstration sings the short form with one friend, because the full verse at the card's finger pace overran 75 s (the first attempt failed: not finished in 75 s), so it does not show the moo-moo lines; it passed at 36.1 s. The moo-moo melody (G G C C C / G G C / C C C G A G) was written from memory of the tune's shape, not a printed score (open in [DEVICE-CHECKS](../DEVICE-CHECKS.md)). Checks are in [VERIFICATION](VERIFICATION-2026-10-13.md#2026-10-13--sing-along-barn-level-6-the-whole-old-macdonald-verse).

### Sing-Along Barn third tune: Mary Had a Little Lamb (2026-10-13)

Added *Mary Had a Little Lamb* (`mary`) to `TUNES` in `sing-along-barn`; no new level, so lap, toddler, preschool and pre-K keep their ranges. It is a seeded draw at levels 1–4 and a third card at levels 5–6. The opening is "Mary had a little lamb" plus "its fleece was white as snow" (13 notes); each friend's verse is "little lamb, little lamb" plus the fleece line (12 notes), and the friend answers on the last "lamb". Notes E D C D E E E / D D D E G G / E D D E D C, all pentatonic, written from the tune's standard form, not a printed score (open in [DEVICE-CHECKS](../DEVICE-CHECKS.md)). Code in `logic.ts` (`MARY_HAD_A`, `LITTLE_LAMB`, `FLEECE`, `MARY`, `tunesFor`), `index.ts`, the how-to card and `barn.sing.mary` / `barn.done.mary`. Still proposals: Au Clair de la Lune, a repeated-phrase mode. Checks are in [VERIFICATION](VERIFICATION-2026-10-13.md#2026-10-13--sing-along-barn-third-tune-mary-had-a-little-lamb).

The optional treehouse keep action receives the snapshot. All existing levels, creative completion, miss/hint accounting and saves remain. Remaining slice-1 words, colors, bin and lap nudge stay in the deep dive.

Typecheck, ten focused Stamp Studio tests and one filtered level-4 regression play passed. The seven lifecycle tests first failed on old behavior; portrait reviewed. No broad suites or build run for this isolated game fix.


### Letter Trails capital starts and rule guards (2026-10-11)

Built deep-dive slice 1 ([PR #172](https://github.com/rjdunlap/neo/pull/172)). A traces downward legs separately; M and N begin at the top and retain continuous paths. Retraced uprights wait for the finger to reverse before consuming the return path, preventing an on-trail drag from receiving a stray miss. Plans, seeded word choice, tolerance, name normalization and the pinned sticker alphabet now live in `logic.ts`. Levels 7–8 speak the word/name introduction, with bounded how-to lines. All existing levels, band ranges, names' 40-letter cap and miss/hint accounting remain; word pictures and per-letter counting are next.


### Goodnight Room barn and animals (2026-10-10)

Built roadmap deep-dive slices 2–3. Levels 1–4 now draw a bedroom or barn from the seeded room pool, with six spacious slots. The barn has a cow, pig, duck, bunny, barn cat and hanging lantern, all drawn in code. Existing names, ordered requests, asleep starts and the finale work in either setting.

Level 5 asks for the living animals in any order, in a bedroom with kitten, puppy and fish plus lamp and clock. It excludes the ambiguous teddy. A wrong object gets a gentle boing and spoken category hint; two misses glow a remaining animal. After the animals settle, the objects settle together, and the shell awards one sticker. Preschool grows to 3–5; pre-K opens at 5. Younger levels keep their saved numbers and ranges.

- Rule tests cover room variety, unique friends, request membership, category fairness, alternative orders and the bot's remaining target. How-to steps are bounded to the right levels.
- Verification is recorded in [VERIFICATION](../VERIFICATION.md); iPad touch, device speech and a child's response stay open.

Existing steering, pattern hints and pond finish are reused, and saved levels keep their meaning. The how-to and spoken instruction explain the new choice.

A code-drawn flower garden counts the days. Every level has a ghost-finger demonstration, scoped how-to and spoken support; co-play and off-screen card play are included.
